- **application-info**: The command can be executed on applications running in both standalone and client/server environments. The information returned depends on where **Application info** is executed. For example, if executed from a remote 4D, the information applies only to the remote workstation. If the command is called in a method that is executed on the server, the information returned applies to the the server. ([Ganbin/4d-development-skill](https://github.com/Ganbin/4d-development-skill/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/application-info.md) · ★10)
